# Skills · N · part 48

906 entries, note to notebooklm. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **note**: Capture something you just learned into your Obsidian vault as a well-placed, well-written note in your own voice. Takes a spoken/typed brain-dump, figures out where it belongs in the learning folder, decides whether to create a new note or merge into an existing one, writes it in Nitin's note-taking voice (not AI voice), wires up wikilinks, and updates the parent index hub. Auto-detects two modes: verbatim (you dumped the full content) and expand (you gave a topic + a few points and want it written up). Use when the user says "I just learned X", "add this to my second brain", "note this down… ([nitintf/skills](https://github.com/nitintf/skills/tree/HEAD/brain/skills/note) · ★1)
- **note**: TRIGGER when: the user says /note, "note this down for next session", "leave a note", "remind me next time", "remind me when we resume", or wants to leave themselves a message for a future session. DO NOT TRIGGER when: the user wants to capture a reusable pattern (/learn), update documentation (/document-all), or record project knowledge (wiki/memory.md — that's the Wiki Updater's job). - **note-curator**: 中文笔记自动整理与归档（通用 Agent skill，适用于任何支持工具调用的 Agent，如 Codex、Claude Code、Cursor 等）。当用户给出 URL 让你「整理/消化/总结这个链接」，或给出本地草稿/笔记内容让你「整理归档/归类/存进知识库」，或要求「整理笔记」「归档笔记」「存到我的知识库」「消化这篇文章」时使用。功能包括：抓取链接正文并按知识类/实验类提炼成结构化中文笔记；整理本地草稿并归类；对知识库做全库查重，命中同类旧笔记则合并补充（优先追加、有错才改），未命中则新建（新建前先学习已有笔记排版风格并模仿）；笔记中的概念对已有笔记自动建立双链（Obsidian 用 [[ ]]）。写作遵循人话守则：笔记面向人类阅读、去 AI 腔，上下文过渡与非术语句按中文语境改写；frontmatter 仅 author/created/tags 三字段，tags≤3 且概括本篇。输出语言跟随源文与目标目录（中英双语，可显式指定），目录按知识领域归类。当前内置 Obsidian 适配，架构可扩展到其它笔记软件。 ([Huayudong/note-curator](https://github.com/Huayudong/note-curator/tree/HEAD/plugins/note-curator/skills/note-curator) · ★2 · 2 copies)
